- **amos-sem-workflow**: AMOS 结构方程建模（SEM）一条龙工作流助手，覆盖从原始问卷数据到论文成文的完整链路：数据清洗（缺失值、异常值、反向计分、Mahalanobis 距离）→ 进入 AMOS 前的"达标预检验"（样本量与 N:p 比、正态性偏度峰度、Little's MCAR、Harman 单因子共同方法偏差、Cronbach's α、KMO 与 Bartlett、EFA、CR 组合信度、AVE 收敛效度、√AVE 与 HTMT 区分效度、VIF 多重共线性）→ AMOS 自动绘图（用 AmosGraphics Plugin 的 VB.NET 代码自动画 CFA/SEM 路径图，自动命名误差项与潜变量、自动画协方差）→ 自动分析（AmosEngine 脚本化建模与估计，免手动点击）→ 模型评估（χ²/df、CFI、TLI、IFI、RMSEA、SRMR、GFI 等拟合指标；修正指数 MI；模型比较）→ 中介与调节效应（Bootstrap 偏差校正置信区间、多群组分析、潜变量交互）→ 结果的 APA 格式文字撰写。触发词包括 AMOS、结构方程、SEM、验证性因子分析、CFA、路径分析、中介效应、调节效应、Bootstrap、拟合指标、模型修正、修正指数、MI、组合信度、AVE、区分效度、共同方法偏差、多群组、AmosEngine、Amos 插件、自动绘图、问卷数据分析。 ([maydengximin-sketch/amos-sem-workflow-skill](https://github.com/maydengximin-sketch/amos-sem-workflow-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/amos-sem-workflow) · ★5)

- **amplicon-benchmark-dataset-generation**: Use when building synthetic long-read (PacBio HiFi) amplicon read sets with known ground-truth ASV counts to benchmark ASV clustering, taxonomic assignment, or abundance-estimation workflows. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-benchmark-dataset-generation))
- **amplicon-clustering-benchmarking**: Use when comparing runtime and memory footprint of swarm versions on large dereplicated amplicon datasets, or when validating claimed speedups (e.g., &gt;5x V9, &gt;9x V4 with fastidious mode). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-clustering-benchmarking))
- **amplicon-community-composition-comparison**: Use when you have paired amplicon OTU tables and independent community survey data and need to quantify whether a processing choice (e.g., LULU curation) alters or improves beta-diversity/composition correlations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-community-composition-comparison))
- **amplicon-consensus-sequence-assembly**: Use when you have demultiplexed, barcode- and primer-trimmed Nanopore amplicon FASTQ for one specimen and need to quantify how consensus accuracy scales with read depth or pick an optimal assembly coverage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/amplicon-consensus-sequence-assembly))
- **amplicon-count-table-rarefaction**: Use when preparing a two-column-metadata 16S rRNA ASV count table for differential abundance tools that require equal library sizes (rarefied input), so all samples share the same total read count after subsampling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-count-table-rarefaction))
- **amplicon-cycle-reconstruction**: Use when low-coverage WGS FASTQ/BAM files from cancer samples (e.g. SRA SRP081035, CCLE PRJNA523380) must be converted into a catalog of eccDNA coordinates (chromosome, start, end, copy count), as in the eccDNAdb study of 3395 tumor samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/amplicon-cycle-reconstruction))
- **amplicon-dada2-truncation-parameter-optimization**: Use when processing paired-end amplicon FASTQ (e.g. 16S rRNA) with DADA2 and the truncation lengths are undetermined or dataset-specific: quality profiles, the need to maximize read retention of target taxa (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-dada2-truncation-parameter-optimization))
- **amplicon-error-background-modeling**: Use when building or validating a background error model for amplicon variant calling: one-mismatch child abundances (mean/SD per cluster) must be related to parent sequencing depth to justify an abundance threshold. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/amplicon-error-background-modeling))
- **amplicon-error-model-denoising**: Use when raw paired-end Illumina FASTQ amplicon reads (e.g. mitochondrial 16S rRNA or COI metabarcoding) must be denoised into per-marker MOTU count tables with error modelling and chimera removal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-error-model-denoising))
- **amplicon-feature-table-rarefaction**: Use when downstream alpha/beta diversity comparisons would be confounded by unequal sequencing depth: an otutab.txt with heterogeneous sample column sums precedes diversity analysis in the EasyAmplicon pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-feature-table-rarefaction))
- **amplicon-length-distribution-quality-control**: Use when full-length 16S rRNA reference sequences (e.g., Greengenes v13.8.99 FASTA) must be trimmed into per-sub-region in-silico amplicons and length-QC'd before benchmarking how well short-read variable regions match full-gene taxonomic resolution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-length-distribution-quality-control))
- **amplicon-metabarcoding-analysis**: Use when analyzing 16S, 18S, ITS, or other amplicon or eDNA metabarcoding reads: ASVs, taxonomy, controls, contamination, reporting. Not for shotgun metagenomes (metagenomics) or sampling planning (sample-and-assay-planning). ([oumarkme/research_agents](https://github.com/oumarkme/research_agents/tree/HEAD/plugins/bioinfo-lab/skills/amplicon-metabarcoding-analysis))
- **amplicon-metabarcoding-data-processing**: Use when an OTU table from VSEARCH, SWARM, CROP or DADA2 inflates richness relative to ground-truth survey data and you need to remove artefactual OTUs before regressing OTU richness against environmental or survey-based richness. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-metabarcoding-data-processing))
- **amplicon-metagenome-profile-agreement-analysis**: Use when paired amplicon (16S) and metagenomic profiles exist for the same mock or known communities and you need to test whether GCN correction (or another bias correction) brings the amplicon profile closer to the metagenomic or expected composition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/amplicon-metagenome-profile-agreement-analysis))
- **amplicon-metagenomic-preprocessing**: Use when raw amplicon FASTQ reads (454 or Illumina 16S/ITS) need primer trimming plus adaptive quality filtering before OTU clustering, and reads exceeding a maximum expected error (EE) budget must be discarded. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/amplicon-metagenomic-preprocessing))
- **amplicon-metagenomic-read-simulation**: Use when a validation study needs communities of known composition to benchmark amplicon profiling or GCN correction — e.g. to test whether CopyRighter-corrected 16S profiles match expected and metagenomic profiles. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/amplicon-metagenomic-read-simulation))
- **amplicon-metagenomic-workflow-command-execution**: Use when you have an OTU representative-sequence FASTA file from MICCA de novo clustering and need an aligned set and phylogenetic tree (Newick) for downstream phylogenetic diversity metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/amplicon-metagenomic-workflow-command-execution))
- **amplicon-naive-bayes-classifier-training**: Use when marker-gene amplicon sequences must be classified to species level with a naive Bayes classifier, and the sample's source environment (e.g. an EMPO 3 habitat) is known so taxonomic class weights can replace the uniform-weight assumption. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-naive-bayes-classifier-training))
- **amplicon-naive-bayes-taxonomy-assignment**: Use when OTU representative sequences need taxonomic labels under alternative 16S reference databases (Greengenes, SILVA, NCBI RefSeq) to quantify how database choice affects downstream community-composition inferences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/amplicon-naive-bayes-taxonomy-assignment))
- **amplicon-otu-asv-method-differentiation**: Use when raw 16S rRNA amplicon sequencing reads must be converted into OTUs or ASVs and the practitioner must decide between (or document both) the 97%-identity clustering route and the denoising ASV route. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-otu-asv-method-differentiation))
- **amplicon-otu-clustering**: Use when you have a dereplicated amplicon FASTA (each entry labeled with its abundance, e.g. seqID_1000) and need high-resolution, input-order-resilient OTU clustering of datasets from thousands up to tera-scale (hundreds of millions of unique sequences). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-otu-clustering))
- **amplicon-otu-clustering-method-comparison**: Use when demultiplexed 16S amplicon reads must be processed by multiple OTU-assignment/denoising methods and the similarity of the resulting representative sequence sets needs to be quantified with UniFrac distances. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/amplicon-otu-clustering-method-comparison))
- **amplicon-per-base-quality-profile-analysis**: Use when raw paired-end FASTQ reads (e.g. Illumina 16S amplicon data) need DADA2 truncation parameters, or when Tourmaline's qaqc trimming-parameter automation must be reproduced or verified from quality profiles. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-per-base-quality-profile-analysis))
- **amplicon-phylo-tree**: Build defensible, publishable phylogenetic trees from second-generation amplicon data (16S/18S, ITS, nifH/amoA/COI and other marker genes): parameters are fixed per study (no hard-wired thresholds), then input audit, alignment, tree inference, rooting and acceptance, producing the tree, the alignment, the QC record and bilingual (Chinese and English) methods paragraphs. Use when the user asks to build a phylogenetic tree from amplicon data, run MAFFT / FastTree / IQ-TREE, make a phylo tree from ASVs or OTUs, check or root an existing tree, or diagnose a broken one. Chinese trigger words: 建树,… ([mumu-sci/amplicon-phylo-tree](https://github.com/mumu-sci/amplicon-phylo-tree/tree/HEAD/skills/amplicon-phylo-tree) · ★1)
- **amplicon-pipeline-combination-enumeration**: Use when assessing how alternative tools/parameters at each 16S pipeline step (DC, CC, TA, OP, NI) affect the inferred co-occurrence network, or when quantifying which step contributes most to total network variation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/amplicon-pipeline-combination-enumeration))
- **amplicon-primer-sequence-trimming**: Use when merged paired-end 16S amplicon FASTQ reads still carry primer bases (e.g. 29 bp forward and 18 bp reverse in EasyAmplicon) and must be trimmed and quality-filtered before dereplication and OTU/ASV inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-primer-sequence-trimming))

- **amplicon-processing**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-/tree/HEAD/Skills/Microbiome/bioSkills/amplicon-processing) · ★36 · 3 copies)
- **amplicon-processing-gptomics-bioskills**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/analysis/amplicon-processing-gptomics-bioskills) · ★745)
- **amplicon-processing-gptomics-bioskills-2**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-ml/amplicon-processing-gptomics-bioskills-2) · ★745)
- **amplicon-qc-workflow-configuration**: Use when configuring Tourmaline/QIIME 2 amplicon QC: paired-end Illumina 16S FASTQ needs DADA2/Deblur 3' truncation lengths derived from FastQC/MultiQC per-base median quality (dropoff below 0.90 of maximum) instead of manual guesses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-qc-workflow-configuration))
- **amplicon-rarefaction-depth-analysis**: Use when validating OTU-count accuracy of a 16S amplicon pipeline against a ground-truth synthetic dataset with known OTU composition, or when comparing de novo OTU-picking pipelines across sequencing depths. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/amplicon-rarefaction-depth-analysis))
- **amplicon-read-alignment-to-reference-otus**: Use when you have a quality-filtered amplicon FASTA (e.g., temp/filtered.fa from vsearch --fastx_filter) and need an OTU table by aligning reads against a USEARCH-compatible reference such as Greengenes 97% OTUs for downstream taxonomy or PICRUSt functional prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-read-alignment-to-reference-otus))
- **amplicon-read-count-aggregation**: Use when you have per-sample classified sequence-variant read counts over a reference taxonomy plus a habitat metadata category (e.g., EMPO 3), and you need habitat-specific taxonomic weights for q2-feature-classifier. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-read-count-aggregation))
- **amplicon-read-count-homogenization-analysis**: Use when you have pre- and post-LULU OTU tables from multiple clustering methods (VSEARCH, SWARM, CROP, DADA2) and need to verify that curation homogenizes taxonomically redundant OTUs without losing total reads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-read-count-homogenization-analysis))
- **amplicon-read-demultiplexing**: Use when raw pooled amplicon FASTQ reads are multiplexed and must be separated into per-sample FASTQ files by barcode before merging, quality filtering, and OTU/ASV feature-table construction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-read-demultiplexing))
- **amplicon-read-dereplication**: Use when quality-filtered FASTQ/FASTA amplicon reads must be collapsed to unique sequences with abundance vectors before de novo OTU inference, chimera filtering, or benchmarking dereplication speed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/amplicon-read-dereplication))
- **amplicon-read-orientation-correction**: Use when INC-Seq circular consensus reads from Oxford Nanopore 16S amplicon runs are inconsistently oriented or span the full insert and must be fragmented and orientation-corrected before nanoClust clustering or blastn-based accuracy assessment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/amplicon-read-orientation-correction))
- **amplicon-read-preprocessing**: Use when raw paired-end amplicon fastq files (16S, ITS, etc.) need to be turned into denoised, chimera-checked ASV tables with taxonomy, on a laptop or a cluster with limited cores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-read-preprocessing))
- **amplicon-read-preprocessing-and-otu-clustering**: Use when raw paired-end .fastq.gz amplicon reads need preprocessing (quality trimming, paired-end assembly, dereplication, chimera removal) and OTU clustering or ASV inference before taxonomic assignment and downstream diversity analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-read-preprocessing-and-otu-clustering))
- **amplicon-read-primer-removal**: Use when raw paired-end amplicon FASTQ files (e.g. 16S V4 with 515F/806R or ITS2 with F-ITS4/R-fITS7) still contain primer sequences at read ends and must be cleaned before DADA2 quality filtering and ASV inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-read-primer-removal))
- **amplicon-read-qc**: Use when raw paired-end amplicon FASTQ files (e.g. seq/Sample1_1.fq.gz + Sample1_2.fq.gz) must be merged, primer-trimmed, and quality-filtered to an expected-error threshold before dereplication, OTU/ASV inference, or chimera removal in EasyAmplicon. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-read-qc))
- **amplicon-read-quality-filtering**: Use when raw 16S rRNA amplicon FASTQ reads (e.g. Illumina MiSeq, ~300 bp paired-end) need per-sample quality assessment, read counting, and denoising into ASVs prior to taxonomy assignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-read-quality-filtering))
- **amplicon-read-quality-filtering**: Use when raw 16S rRNA amplicon sequencing reads (e.g., FASTQ deposited in QIITA) must be quality-filtered in QIIME 2.0 before downstream feature table, taxonomy, and phylogeny generation for MiSurv-style survival analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/amplicon-read-quality-filtering))
- **amplicon-read-quality-preprocessing**: Use when you have raw paired-end .fastq.gz amplicon reads from Illumina sequencing for 16S/18S rRNA, ITS, COI, or 12S rRNA marker genes and need QC, trimming, error correction, assembly, dereplication, and chimera removal before OTU/ASV analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-read-quality-preprocessing))
- **amplicon-read-to-reference-alignment**: Use when real or MHASS-simulated PacBio HiFi amplicon reads must be aligned to reference ASV sequences to quantify per-read edit-distance distributions or position-specific insertion/substitution/deletion error profiles. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-read-to-reference-alignment))
- **amplicon-reference-sequence-handling**: Use when a QIIME 2 amplicon pipeline needs a species-annotation classifier and a reference database such as Greengenes 13_8 99% OTUs must be downloaded, imported, and trained into a TaxonomicClassifier artifact. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-reference-sequence-handling))
- **amplicon-sequence-chimera-detection**: Use when dereplicated amplicon FASTA reads (e.g. after illuminapairedend alignment, quality/length filtering and obiuniq dereplication) must be screened for chimeric sequences before MOTU clustering, without a reference database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-sequence-chimera-detection))
- **amplicon-sequence-demultiplexing**: Use when starting from raw pooled amplicon sequencing data (per-study demultiplexed raw sequence files from an EMP-style FTP deposit) and per-sample FASTQ tables are needed before OTU picking or Deblur processing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-sequence-demultiplexing))
- **amplicon-sequence-denoising**: Use when comparing DADA2/Deblur denoising against VSEARCH clustering on amplicon paired-end reads, or when deciding whether low-abundance filtering mitigates error-rate differences between ASV and OTU inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-sequence-denoising))
- **amplicon-sequence-denoising-dada2**: Use when raw demultiplexed paired-end Illumina FASTQ reads need error correction and chimera removal into a feature table/representative-sequence pair, with trimming parameters determined automatically from quality scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-sequence-denoising-dada2))
- **amplicon-sequence-dereplication**: Use when filtered/merged amplicon reads (e.g., temp/filtered.fa) must be de-redundized and reduced to a representative OTU or ASV set before building an OTU table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-sequence-dereplication))
- **amplicon-sequence-environment-specificity-analysis**: Use when you have a large multi-study amplicon feature table (e.g., Deblur tag sequences or OTU BIOM tables) plus sample metadata with environment categories, and you need to determine at which phylogenetic resolution habitat specificity is captured. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-sequence-environment-specificity-analysis))
- **amplicon-sequence-similarity-matching**: Use when an OTU table plus its representative centroid sequences (e.g. otutable_test.txt and centroids_test.txt) must be converted into a match list of internal OTU similarities before running LULU curation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-sequence-similarity-matching))
- **amplicon-sequence-variant-cluster-analysis**: Use when evaluating or reproducing an inference test on one-mismatch children in HashSeq cluster output (OneMismatchCluster.txt, childrenProperties.txt), e.g. to benchmark Poisson-based variant-calling error models. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/amplicon-sequence-variant-cluster-analysis))
- **amplicon-sequence-variant-fetching-via-redbiom**: Use when a downstream step (e.g., q2-clawback assemble_weights_from_Qiita) needs the sequence variants of samples sharing a metadata category value (e.g., an EMPO 3 habitat) and those samples live in Qiita. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-sequence-variant-fetching-via-redbiom))
- **amplicon-sequencing-simulation**: Use when you need synthetic PacBio HiFi amplicon data (e.g. 16S reads) from known genomes to benchmark ASV clustering or taxonomic assignment, or to compare simulated vs real mock-community abundance correlations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/amplicon-sequencing-simulation))
- **amplicon-snv-variant-calling**: Use when targeted nanopore amplicon reads (e.g. CYP2D6, HLA-A, HLA-B loci) must be mapped to a reference and SNVs called, and you need to quantify how the choice of mapper affects variant-calling precision and sensitivity against a truth set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/amplicon-snv-variant-calling))
- **amplicon-survey-detection-bias-analysis**: Use when you have curated 16S rRNA gene sequences (e.g. assembled and intron-stripped) and need to estimate which taxa a specific primer pair would fail to amplify in a cultivation-independent amplicon survey. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/amplicon-survey-detection-bias-analysis))
